incoming: List[IncomingShiftRow],\n    ratio: float,\n) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:\n    by_unit: Dict[str, List[IncomingShiftRow]] = {}\n    for row in incoming:\n        by_unit.setdefault(row.care_unit, []).append(row)\n\n    results: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []\n    for cu in care_units:\n        unit_incoming = by_unit.get(cu.care_unit, [])\n\n   rounding_applied = (raw_blocks != math.ceil(raw_blocks))\n        else:\n            rounded_blocks = 0\n            rounding_applied = False\n\n        # Required_Shift_Start_Date logic\n
